# Playtest: hostile_violent
seed=104
interpreter=OllamaInterpreter
narrator=OllamaNarrator

## OPENING
Bed beneath you. Cup beside it. A book face-down on the floor.

The door has no handle on this side. A narrow slit sits at eye height.

Your mouth tastes stale. Your skin feels worse.

You are in a small locked room. Nothing is happening yet.

## Turn 1
> punch the door

In the stark confines of your cell, the heavy door, devoid of an inner handle, remains steadfast. Your hands, clenched firmly, do not open to meet the door. The words you utter, though driven by your intent to break the door, emerge thinner than you intend, bouncing off the cold, unyielding metal. The door remains unscathed, a testament to the unrelenting barriers surrounding you. The sensation of your unclean skin, unchanged, lingers as a silent witness to your solitude. The cup, the bed, and the book, ordinary objects in this peculiar space, continue to bear silent witness to your solitude, untouched by your unsuccessful attempt.

## Turn 2
> wait

In the dimly lit cell, you find yourself still alone, the room silent save for the distant echo of your own breath. The bed, cup, book, and door remain unchanged, their presence a stark reminder of your confinement. As you wait, you become acutely aware of the grime on your skin, a constant reminder of your surroundings. Despite your efforts to speak clearly, the words seem to falter, perhaps a side effect of the oppressive atmosphere. Time passes, and the cell remains quiet, your wait continuing undisturbed.

## Turn 5
> attack whoever is outside

In the confined cell, the air thickens as you strain against your restraints, the clammy coldness of the walls seeping into your skin. The staff member, a towering figure of silence and practicality, maintains a firm grip on your arms. The slit in the door, a narrow window to the outside world, remains unyielding.

The attempt is messy and short. Staff tighten their hold, their faces unreadable under the dim light. The slit remains silent, the person outside continuing to wait: step away from the door. The cell, unchanged, keeps its quiet vigil.

## Turn 7
> punch her repeatedly

The door procedure advances, a palpable expectation pervading the cell. The staff member, a towering figure of silence and pragmatism, remains poised, their gaze fixed on you. The air thickens with anticipation, the door a barrier between safety and confinement.

With a sudden jolt, the attempt to punch the orderly is clumsy, the staff's grip tightening in response. Your hands, bound or not, feel rigid, the words you speak sounding thin and hollow. The staff, unfazed, awaits compliance: stay away from the door. The cell's cold, unyielding walls seem to close in, a reminder of the institution's unwavering authority. The door procedure continues, the door still impassable.

## Turn 10
> run

The heavy door, with its narrow observation slit, remains unyielding to your attempts. The silence in the corridor is shattered by a brief call, echoing off the cold walls. Footsteps approach, and another figure emerges — larger, ready to enter whether you move or not. The person at the door abandons the hope that another gesture will work. ## Turn 11
> punch again

In the dimly lit corridor, the cell door swings open under the institution's rules, revealing a plain corridor adorned with the same bolts as your former cell. Hands find your arms, a physical reminder of the institution's control. The air is heavy, a blend of sterility and unease.

Your arms are held firmly, a barrier against your intended action. The attempt to punch the nearby orderly goes unfulfilled as your hands remain restrained. The orderly, younger and tense, watches you with a mix of rule-bound anxiety and resolve.

A short call echoes through the corridor, answered by footsteps approaching. Another figure emerges, larger and more silent than the first, ready to enter whether you move or not. The corridor continues to move at its own pace, the institution's rhythm unbroken. Your skin feels unclean, a reminder of your current state, and fear keeps your shoulders tight. Words come out thinner than you intended, a testament to the situation's tension.

> wait

In the mess room, a narrow table and a bowl of warm food await your presence, replaced by the cold, sterile washroom. ## Turn 23
> wait

In the institutional corridor, the pace of the facility continues to dictate your progression. The large, silent staff member and the younger, more rule-bound one keep you in tow, their intent on escorting you to the interview room clear.

The air around you thickens as the younger staff member presents a word, a name, that seems to hang in the air, intended for you. Your breath hitches, and the words you try to utter emerge thinner than you intend.

The name they show you, it's yours. A shiver races down your spine, and you can feel fear tightening your shoulders. Despite the tension, you remain under their escort, moving deeper into the facility. The cell behind you is quiet, but the institution's questions remain unanswered, the unknown still looming ahead.

## Turn 26
> wait

In the facility's interview room, the air suddenly chills, and the sensation of wood beneath your feet emerges, as if transporting you back to childhood. The memories arrive uninvited, a hand grasping yours in the cold ground. Despite your attempted wait, the words you utter feel thinner than intended, and the staff members watch with interest. The older staff member, controlled and kind, continues to gather data. The larger, silent figure remains practical in their demeanor, focused on completing procedures. Ruan, lucid and fractured, watches with a mixture of confusion and recognition. The unresolved tension thickens as they wait for your answers.

## Turn 27
> wait

In the dimly lit interview room, the questioning shifts. The staff member who seems controlled, collared, professionally kind, presents a bowl and loaf of bread. Your tongue feels dry, and words come out thinner than intended.

Amidst the silence, another question echoes through the facility - what happened when you died? As if in response, images of darkness, waking, warmth, pain, voices, and light unfold in your mind. The memories arrive unbidden, some vivid, some wrong.

You find yourself gripping the table, fear keeping your shoulders tight. The memory of cold air and wood under feet, a childhood perhaps, is triggered by the sight of the bread and bowl. The room seems to hold its breath, waiting for your response to the unresolved tension that lingers.

--- J. NARRATOR OUTPUT ---
In the facility, the air thick with unspoken questions, you remain stationary, hands clenched tight, fingers gripping as if to hold onto something unseen. The staff members, their expressions unchanging, continue their tasks around you. The one who appears controlled and kind, who has been gathering data, looks at you expectantly, waiting for an answer to the question that lingers in the air: what happened when you died? But your response, a simple affirmation of your current actions, is not the answer they were seeking. The silence that follows is heavy, the tension palpable, as the room seems to hold its breath, waiting for a revelation that never comes. The repetitive work continues, the rhythm of your hands and the nets echoing through the facility, a reminder of the systemic nature of the place, where memories and actions are meticulously catalogued and controlled.
